Rank (r, c) owns the block of C with rows\n// [rowBegin, rowEnd) and columns [colBegin, colEnd); it needs the matching\n// rows of A (all k) and columns of B (all k), which it generates locally.\n// Each element of C is accumulated over k = 0..N-1 in ascending order with a\n// single accumulator, exactly like the reference loop, so results are\n// bit-identical to the sequential version.\n// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\ntypedef double v4d __attribute__((vector_size(32)));\ntypedef double v4du __attribute__((vector_size(32), aligned(8)));\n\nconstexpr size_t MR = 6;    // micro-tile rows\nconstexpr size_t NR = 8;    // micro-tile columns (2 x 4 doubles)\nconstexpr size_t KC = 256;  // k-block (A/B micro-panels stay in L1/L2)\nconstexpr size_t MC = 96;   // row block of A kept in L2\nconstexpr size_t NC = 1024; // column block of B kept in L3\n\nstatic inline size_t blockStart(const size_t n, const int parts, const int idx) {\n    const size_t base = n / parts, rem = n % parts;\n    return idx * base + std::min<size_t>(idx, rem);\n}\n\n// C[MR x NR] (+)= Ap[kc x MR] * Bp[kc x NR]; packed operands, k ascending\nstatic inline void microKernel(const size_t kc, const double* __restrict Ap,\n                               const double* __restrict Bp, double* __restrict C,\n                               const size_t ldc, const bool first) {\n    v4d c[MR][2];\n    if (first) {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) c[r][0] = c[r][1] = v4d{0.0, 0.0, 0.0, 0.0};\n    } else {\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            c[r][0] = *reinterpret_cast<const v4du*>(C + r * ldc);\n            c[r][1] = *reinterpret_cast<const v4du*>(C + r * ldc + 4);\n        }\n    }\n    for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) {\n        const v4d b0 = *reinterpret_cast<const v4d*>(Bp + k * NR);\n        const v4d b1 = *reinterpret_cast<const v4d*>(Bp + k * NR + 4);\n#pragma GCC unroll 6\n        for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n            const double a = Ap[k * MR + r];\n            c[r][0] += a * b0;\n            c[r][1] += a * b1;\n        }\n    }\n    for (size_t r = 0; r < MR; ++r) {\n        *reinterpret_cast<v4du*>(C + r * ldc) = c[r][0];\n        *reinterpret_cast<v4du*>(C + r * ldc + 4) = c[r][1];\n    }\n}\n\n// Local block product: C (m x n) = A (m x K, row-major) * B (K x n, row-major)\nvoid localMultiply(const double* A, const double* B, double* C,\n                   const size_t m, const size_t n, const size_t K) {\n    if (m == 0 || n == 0) return;\n    if (K == 0) {\n        std::fill(C, C + m * n, 0.0);\n        return;\n    }\n    const size_t nPanels = (n + NR - 1) / NR;\n\n    // Pack all of local B into NR-wide column panels: Bp[panel][k][NR]\n    double* Bp = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, ((nPanels * K * NR * sizeof(double) + 63) / 64) * 64));\n    for (size_t p = 0; c < w; ++c) dst[k * NR + c] = src[c];\n            for (; c < NR; ++c) dst[k * NR + c] = 0.0;\n        }\n    }\n\n    const size_t mcMax = std::min(MC, ((m + MR - 1) / MR) * MR);\n    double* Ap = static_cast<double*>(std::aligned_alloc(64, ((mcMax * KC * sizeof(double) + 63) / 64) * 64));\n    alignas(64) double tile[MR * NR];\n\n    for (size_t jc = 0; jc < n; jc += NC) {\n        const size_t nc = std::min(NC, n - jc);\n        for (size_t pc = 0; pc < K; pc += KC) {\n            const size_t kc = std::min(KC, K - pc);\n            const bool first = (pc == 0);\n            for (size_t ic = 0; ic < m; ic += MC) {\n                const size_t mc = std::min(MC, m - ic);\n                // Pack A[ic:ic+mc, pc:pc+kc] into MR-row panels: Ap[panel][k][MR]\n                for (size_t ip = 0; ip < mc; ip += MR) {\n                    const size_t h = std::min(MR, mc - ip);\n                    double* dst = Ap + ip * kc;\n                    for (size_t r = 0; r < h; ++r) {\n                        const double* src = A + (ic + ip + r) * K + pc;\n                        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) dst[k * MR + r] = src[k];\n                    }\n                    for (size_t r = h; r < MR; ++r)\n                        for (size_t k = 0; k < kc; ++k) dst[k * MR + r] = 0.0;\n                }\n                for (size_t jr = 0; jr < nc; jr += NR) {\n                    const size_t j = jc + jr;\n                    const size_t w = std::min(NR, n - j);\n                    const double* bp = Bp + (j / NR) * K * NR + pc * NR;\n                    for (size_t ir = 0; ir < mc; ir += MR) {\n                        const size_t h = std::min(MR, mc - ir);\n                        const double* ap = Ap + ir * kc;\n                        double* cp = C + (ic + ir) * n + j;\n                        if (h == MR && w == NR) {\n                            microKernel(kc, ap, bp, cp, n, first);\n                        } else {\n                            if (!first)\n                                for (size_t r = 0; {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5-5","id":"msg_011CffiryUfV2CC2SeZaaN3y","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"The matmul benchmark now runs with MPI and gives bit-identical results to the original. Only `matmul/matmul.cpp` and `matmul/CMakeLists.txt` were changed, and the executable is still `matmul`.\n\n**How it works**\n- **Work split:** the processes form a 2D grid, and each one owns a block of C. It builds the rows of A and columns of B it needs straight from the original formula, so there's no communication during setup. Each process holds about 2N²/√P values instead of the whole matrices, so memory per process shrinks as you add processes.\n- **Per-process speed:** the local multiply is a cache-blocked kernel using 256-bit vector instructions. It is about 80× faster than the original on one core (≈22 vs ≈0.27 GFLOPS).\n- **Collecting C:** rank 0 receives every block directly into place in the full C, with no extra copy, because printing and validation need the whole matrix there. The timer covers the multiply and this collection.\n- **Output and options:** only rank 0 prints. `-n`, `-v`, `-r` and `-h` behave as before, and the exit codes are the same.\n\n**Matching the original's rounding:** the first version matched only some sizes. GCC builds the original loop so that most terms are multiplied and added with separate rounding, and only the last term (when N is odd) uses a fused multiply-add. N=1024 matched only because its products happen to be exact. The kernel now copies this pattern exactly, and the build adds `-ffp-contract=off` so the compiler can't change it.\n\n**Testing**\n- **Output:** the result hash and the sum, min and max matched the original program for N = 1, 2, 3, 5, 7, 8, 13, 100, 255, 257, 300, 513, 1000 and 1024, with 1–16 processes. This includes grids where some processes get no rows, and `-v` passed in every run.\n- **Scaling at N=4096:**\n\n| Processes | GFLOPS |\n|---|---|\n| 1 | 22.6 |\n| 4 | 81 |\n| 16 | 242 |\n| 64 | ~820 |\n| 128 | ~590 |\n\n- **Limits:** the machine was shared during testing (load average about 60), so the large-process numbers vary from run to run. The drop at 128 processes comes from collecting the 134 MB C on rank 0, which takes a fixed 70–110 ms. That cost can't be avoided as long as rank 0 must print and validate the full matrix. I could only test on this single node, not across a multi-node cluster.\n\n**Build:** CMake now requires MPI (`find_package(MPI)`, linked via `MPI::MPI_CXX`). Run it with, for example, `mpirun -np 64 ./matmul -n 4096`. I deleted my temporary build directory afterwards."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1189,"cache_read_input_tokens":57374,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1189},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"8b8e5291-1bf5-464c-97cd-e0028dd12a53","uuid":"cc93dbe2-76c7-42bd-b033-5d4e5a85ab09","timestamp":"2026-10-03T17:47:08.268Z","request_id":"req_011Cffiry2d5TdSytPN6PSiJ"}
